| --- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 3   | Emoticon picker dimensions — panel height, grid density | Emoticons (§ 13.6.) | #2 decided the assets are user-authored, so the grid must hold **arbitrary** aspect ratios rather than a known set — a fixed square cell with the image `object-contain` inside it. § 13.6. has since landed with a half-shell panel and a square cell; **confirm the density reads right before closing this** |

**Decided — kept for the record, do not reopen without asking**

| #   | Question                                              | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1   | A chat message when its image is deleted from library | **One act, open to either participant.** 삭제 destroys the object; the bubble keeps its place and draws `삭제된 사진이에요`, exactly as § 8.13. keeps a withdrawn message in place. The row survives — `message_media`'s FK does not cascade — and the geometry survives with it so the tombstone fills the box the picture did. It is not the uploader's alone: 보관함 is the shared album, so throwing something out of it belongs to both, and what makes that answerable is that the act never removes a bubble — only the picture inside one. `isInLibrary()` is the sole guard on `destroyArchiveMedia` and is what keeps it away from an avatar or a profile cover. **The reversible half was removed** (`0043`): 보관함에서 숨기기, `archive_hidden_at` and the library-only upload's `archive_added_at` are all gone, and every value they held was carried over to `deleted_at`. Membership is now "a live message carries it" and nothing else — which is what makes 보관함 exactly what the conversation exchanged, and what makes a withdrawn message take its media off the shelf. One cost was accepted for that: an upload whose POST fails lands nowhere. The other — a withdrawn message leaving rows that no screen reaches and no column marks — is closed by `deleteMessage` stamping the media it carried in the same transaction (§ 8.13.), skipping any object another live message still holds. **That stamp enqueues the bytes too** (§ 9.): the objects are reclaimed once `MEDIA_DELETE_GRACE` has passed, on the same grace every media-scope delete takes. This used to say the objects were deliberately left, on the argument that withdrawing is the sender's act while destroying a shared object belongs to both — **the retention that argument bought was imaginary**, because nothing anywhere in the app clears `deleted_at`. There is no un-delete, so a withdrawal was never reversible and the bytes were only ever waiting for a sweep to notice them |
| 2   | How emoticon assets are sourced and packs composed    | **Authored in the app** (§ 13.). Aspect ratios are therefore arbitrary, which is what #3 must absorb                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| 4   | Calendar event color set                              | **A closed set of six** — `clay` / `honey` / `olive` / `teal` / `blue` / `plum` (DESIGN § 4.1.7.). Three are cool deliberately; `primary` is excluded                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| 5   | Motion duration / easing token scale                  | **Five durations and three curves** (DESIGN § 4.7.). The 1.5s search flash and Vaul's own sheet timing sit outside the scale                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 8   | Whether to adopt push notifications                   | **Adopted**, new-message only (§ 16.1.). **Time-based notifications remain out of scope**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 9   | Whether a `scope = 'mine'` event shows its title      | **The title is shown.** Hiding it would make `scope` a privacy control, which § 11.5. says it is not. The ring dot is the whole distinction                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| 10  | Maximum images per message                            | **Selection is unlimited; a send splits into consecutive bubbles of 9.** No `+N` overflow cell, no hidden attachment. Caps are per file (§ 9.)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| 6   | Viewer **pinch-zoom** bounds                          | **1x–4x, double tap toggling 2x**, rubber-banding past either bound (§ 8.1.). 4x is what makes text in a screenshot readable, which is the common case. The swipe half was already settled — native scroll snapping, no threshold. Still wants confirming on a real device; the three constants sit at the top of `usePinchZoom`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| 7   | Dark palette hex values                               | **Hand-tuned in `theme.css`'s `.dark` block**, never arithmetically inverted, no neutral at `#000000` (DESIGN.md § 5.4.). Every pair in use clears WCAG AA. Two reversals the light theme forces and worth remembering: `on-primary` inverts to dark because a `primary` light enough to pass as a link fails white text on its own fill, and `chat-sender` becomes **lighter** than `chat-meta` where light made it darker                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
